class="tocCollapsible_ldQs theme-doc-toc-mobile tocMobile_xBih"><button type="button" class="clean-btn tocCollapsibleButton_Dk1U">On this page</button></div><div class="theme-doc-markdown markdown">\<header\>\<h1\>Views</h1></header><p>Views are read-only functions that compute and return results from your tables. Unlike <a class="" href="/docs/functions/reducers">reducers</a>, views do not modify database state - they only query and return data. Views are useful for computing derived data, aggregations, or joining multiple tables before sending results to clients.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="why-use-views">Why Use Views?<a href="#why-use-views" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Why Use Views?" title="Direct link to Why Use Views?" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Views provide several benefits:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><strong>Performance</strong>: Views compute results server-side, reducing the amount of data sent to clients</li>
<li class=""><strong>Encapsulation</strong>: Views can hide complex queries behind simple interfaces</li>
<li class=""><strong>Consistency</strong>: Views ensure clients receive consistently formatted data</li>
<li class=""><strong>Real-time updates</strong>: Like tables, views can be subscribed to and automatically update when underlying data changes</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="defining-views">Defining Views<a href="#defining-views" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Defining Views" title="Direct link to Defining Views" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Views must be declared as <code>public</code> with an explicit <code>name</code>. They take a context parameter and can also accept <a href="#views-with-arguments" class="">user-defined arguments</a> in TypeScript, C#, and Rust.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>Use the <code>spacetimedb.view</code> or <code>spacetimedb.anonymousView</code> function:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { schema, table, t } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb/server</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> players </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">players</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">primaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">autoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    identity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unique</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">??</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> undefined</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">players_for_level</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (p) out.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">push</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.id, name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.name, level</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> playerLevel.level });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre><p>The handler signature is <code>(ctx) => rows</code>, or <code>(ctx, args) => rows</code> for <a href="#views-with-arguments" class="">views with arguments</a>, where <code>rows</code> must be either an array or option of product values.</p></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Use the <code>[SpacetimeDB.View]</code> attribute on a static method:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                rows.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Add</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(row);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> rows;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre><p>Views must be static methods and can return either a single row (<code>T?</code>) or multiple rows (<code>IEnumerable\<T\></code>, <code>List\<T\></code>, or <code>T[]</code>) where <code>T</code> can be a table type or any product type.</p></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Use the <code>#[spacetimedb::view]</code> macro on a function:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{view, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, table, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeType</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[primary_key]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[auto_inc]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[unique]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    identity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player_level)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[unique]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    player_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[index(btree)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    level</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[derive(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeType</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerAndLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    identity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    level</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// At-most-one row: return Option\<T\></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> my_player, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> my_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Option</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Multiple rows: return Vec\<T\></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> players_for_level, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> players_for_level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Vec</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerAndLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player_level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">2</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">flat_map</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">                .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">                .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">id</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">                .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">player_id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">                .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">map</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerAndLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">id,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                    identity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">identity,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">name,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                    level</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">level,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">collect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre><p>Views can return either <code>Option\<T\></code> for at-most-one row or <code>Vec\<T\></code> for multiple rows, where <code>T</code> can be a table type or any product type.</p></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Use the <code>SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</code> macro:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">#include</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> <</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb.h</span><span style="color:#E9F284">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> namespace</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> SpacetimeDB;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    Identity identity;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Player, id, identity, name)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Player, player, Public)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_PrimaryKeyAutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player, id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Unique</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player, identity)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player_id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> level;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(PlayerLevel, player_id, level)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(PlayerLevel, player_level, Public)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Unique</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player_level, player_id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player_level, level)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerAndLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> level;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(PlayerAndLevel, id, name, level)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// At-most-one row: return std::optional\<T\></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">optional</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, my_player, Public, ViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[player_identity].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Multiple rows: return std::vector\<T\></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">vector</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">PlayerAndLevel</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, players_for_level, Public, AnonymousViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">vector</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">PlayerAndLevel</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> results;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Find all player levels with level == 2</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player_lv : ctx.db[player_level_level].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">2</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[player_id].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player_lv.player_id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (p) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            results.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">push_back</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(PlayerAndLevel{p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">id, p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">name, player_lv.level});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> results;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre><p>Views can return <code>std::optional\<T\></code> for at-most-one row, <code>std::vector\<T\></code> for multiple rows, or a query-builder type, where <code>T</code> can be a table type or any product type.</p></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="viewcontext-and-anonymousviewcontext">ViewContext and AnonymousViewContext<a href="#viewcontext-and-anonymousviewcontext" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to ViewContext and AnonymousViewContext" title="Direct link to ViewContext and AnonymousViewContext" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Views use one of two context types:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><strong><code>ViewContext</code></strong>: Provides access to the caller&#x27;s <code>Identity</code> through the context&#x27;s sender field or method. Use this when the view depends on who is querying it.</li>
<li class=""><strong><code>AnonymousViewContext</code></strong>: Does not provide caller information. Use this when the view produces the same results regardless of who queries it.</li>
</ul>
<p>Both contexts provide read-only access to tables and indexes through <code>ctx.db</code>.</p>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="performance-why-anonymousviewcontext-matters">Performance: Why AnonymousViewContext Matters<a href="#performance-why-anonymousviewcontext-matters" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Performance: Why AnonymousViewContext Matters" title="Direct link to Performance: Why AnonymousViewContext Matters" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>The choice between <code>ViewContext</code> and <code>AnonymousViewContext</code> has significant performance implications.</p>
<p><strong>Anonymous views can be shared across all subscribers.</strong> When a view uses <code>AnonymousViewContext</code>, SpacetimeDB knows the result is the same for every client. The database can materialize the view once and serve that same result to all subscribers. When the underlying data changes, it recomputes the view once and broadcasts the update to everyone.</p>
<p><strong>Per-user views require separate computation for each subscriber.</strong> When a view uses <code>ViewContext</code> and reads the caller&#x27;s sender identity, each client potentially sees different data. SpacetimeDB must compute and track the view separately for each subscriber. With 1,000 connected users, that&#x27;s 1,000 separate view computations and 1,000 separate sets of change tracking.</p>
<p><strong>Prefer <code>AnonymousViewContext</code> when possible.</strong> Design your views to be caller-independent when the use case allows. For example:</p>
<table><thead><tr><th>Use Case</th><th>Recommended Context</th><th>Why</th></tr></thead>\<tbody\><tr><td>Global leaderboard</td><td><code>AnonymousViewContext</code></td><td>Same top-10 for everyone</td></tr><tr><td>Shop inventory</td><td><code>AnonymousViewContext</code></td><td>Same items available to all</td></tr><tr><td>My inventory</td><td><code>ViewContext</code></td><td>Different per player</td></tr><tr><td>My messages</td><td><code>ViewContext</code></td><td>Private to each user</td></tr><tr><td>World map regions</td><td><code>AnonymousViewContext</code></td><td>Geographic data shared by all nearby players</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><strong>Design around shared data when you can.</strong> Sometimes a small design change lets you use anonymous views. For example, instead of a view that returns "entities near me" (which requires knowing who "me" is), consider views that return "entities in region X". Multiple players in the same region share a single materialized view rather than each having their own.</p>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="example-per-user-view">Example: Per-User View<a href="#example-per-user-view" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Example: Per-User View" title="Direct link to Example: Per-User View" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>This view returns the caller&#x27;s own player data. Each connected client sees different results, so SpacetimeDB must track it separately for each subscriber.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Per-user: each client sees their own player</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> myPlayer </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">view</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my_player</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">option</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(players.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db.players.identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">??</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> undefined</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Per-user: each client sees their own player</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">MyPlayer</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">? </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">MyPlayer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Db.Player.Identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.Sender);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Per-user: each client sees their own player</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> my_player, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> my_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Option</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Per-user: each client sees their own player</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">optional</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, my_player, Public, ViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[player_identity].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="example-high-scores-leaderboard">Example: High Scores Leaderboard<a href="#example-high-scores-leaderboard" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Example: High Scores Leaderboard" title="Direct link to Example: High Scores Leaderboard" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>This view returns players with scores above a threshold. Every client sees the same results, so SpacetimeDB computes it once and shares it across all subscribers. The view uses a btree index on the score column to efficiently find high-scoring players.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> players </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">players</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">primaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">autoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    score</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">btree</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> schema</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ players });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> default</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Shared: same high scorers for all clients</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> highScorers </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">anonymousView</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">high_scorers</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(players.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Get all players with score >= 1000 using the btree index</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Array.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.db.players.score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ gte</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1000</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Player</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Player</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">BTree</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Score;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Shared: same high scorers for all clients</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">HighScorers</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> List</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">HighScorers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Get all players with score >= 1000 using the btree index</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Db.Player.Score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">((</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.MaxValue)).</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ToList</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[primary_key]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[auto_inc]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[index(btree)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    score</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Shared: same high scorers for all clients</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> high_scorers, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> high_scorers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Vec</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Get all players with score >= 1000 using the btree index</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">score</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">..</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">collect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> score;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Player, id, name, score)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Player, player, Public)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_PrimaryKeyAutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player, id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player, score)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Shared: same high scorers for all clients</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">vector</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, high_scorers, Public, AnonymousViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[player_high_scorers_score].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">range_from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))).</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">collect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="example-region-based-design">Example: Region-Based Design<a href="#example-region-based-design" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Example: Region-Based Design" title="Direct link to Example: Region-Based Design" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>Instead of querying "what&#x27;s near me" (per-user), design your data model so clients subscribe to shared regions. This example shows entities organized by chunk coordinates.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> entity </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">entity</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">primaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">autoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    chunkX</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">i32</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">btree</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    chunkY</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">i32</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">btree</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    localX</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">f32</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    localY</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">f32</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    entityType</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Track which chunks each player is subscribed to</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> playerChunk </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">player_chunk</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    playerId</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">primaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    chunkX</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">i32</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    chunkY</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">i32</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Shared: all players in chunk (0,0) share this view</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> entitiesInOriginChunk </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">anonymousView</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">entities_in_origin_chunk</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(entity.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // All entities in chunk (0, 0) - shared by everyone viewing this chunk</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Array.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.db.entity.chunkX.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">e</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> e.chunkY </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">===</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Per-user: returns entities in the chunk the player is currently in</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> entitiesInMyChunk </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">view</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">entities_in_my_chunk</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(entity.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db.players.identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">player) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [];</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> chunk </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db.playerChunk.playerId.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player.id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">chunk) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [];</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Array.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.db.entity.chunkX.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(chunk.chunkX))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">e</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> e.chunkY </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">===</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> chunk.chunkY);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Module</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Entity</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Entity</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">BTree</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> int</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ChunkX;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">BTree</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> int</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ChunkY;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> float</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> LocalX;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> float</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> LocalY;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> EntityType;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Track which chunks each player is subscribed to</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">PlayerChunk</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerChunk</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> PlayerId;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> int</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ChunkX;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> int</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ChunkY;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Shared: all players in chunk (0,0) share this view</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">EntitiesInOriginChunk</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> List</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Entity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">EntitiesInOriginChunk</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">        // All entities in chunk (0, 0) - shared by everyone viewing this chunk</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Db.Entity.ChunkX.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">e</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> e.ChunkY </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ToList</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Per-user: returns entities in the chunk the player is currently in</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_PrimaryKeyAutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(entity, id)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Track which chunks each player is subscribed to</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Shared: all players in chunk (0,0) share this view</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">vector</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Entity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> results;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // All entities in chunk (0, 0) - shared by everyone viewing this chunk</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> entity : ctx.db[entity_chunk_x].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">int32_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))) {</span></span>
