<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            results.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">push_back</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(entity);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> results;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Per-user: returns entities in the chunk the player is currently in</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">vector</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Entity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, entities_in_my_chunk, Public, ViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">vector</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Entity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> results;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Find the player&#x27;s current location</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player_opt </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[player_identity].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">player_opt) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> results;</span><span style="color:#6272A4">  // Player not found</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Find which chunk the player is in</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> chunk_opt </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[player_chunk_player_id].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player_opt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">chunk_opt) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> results;</span><span style="color:#6272A4">  // Player has no chunk assigned</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Return all entities in the player&#x27;s chunk</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> entity : ctx.db[entity_chunk_x].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(chunk_opt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">chunk_x)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (entity.chunk_y </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> chunk_opt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">chunk_y) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            results.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">push_back</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(entity);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> results;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>The <code>entities_in_origin_chunk</code> view is shared - if 100 players are all looking at chunk (0,0), SpacetimeDB computes it once. The <code>entities_in_my_chunk</code> view requires per-user computation since each player may be in a different chunk.</p>
<p>For games with many players in the same area, the shared approach scales much better. Clients can subscribe to the specific chunk views they need based on their position, and players in the same chunk automatically share the same materialized data.</p>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="example-counts">Example: Counts<a href="#example-counts" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Example: Counts" title="Direct link to Example: Counts" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>Procedural views can call <code>count()</code> or <code>Count</code> on a table accessor to read the current number of rows in a table.
This works with both <code>ViewContext</code> and <code>AnonymousViewContext</code>.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> playerCountRow </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">row</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    count</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> playerCount </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">anonymousView</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">player_count</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(playerCountRow),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [{ count</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db.players.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">count</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() }]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerCountRow</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Count;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">PlayerCount</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> List</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">PlayerCount</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> List</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { Count </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Db.Player.Count }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{view, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeType</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[derive(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeType</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    count</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player_count, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> player_count</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Vec</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    vec!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        count</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">count</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> count;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(PlayerCountRow, count)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">optional</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, player_count, Public, AnonymousViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">optional</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerCountRow</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">>(PlayerCountRow{ctx.db[player].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">count</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p><code>count()</code> is a constant-time metadata lookup, so it does not iterate the table.
<code>count()</code> requires re-evaluation whenever any row of the underlying table is updated, however that re-evaluation is still cheap because <code>count()</code> is cheap.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="querying-views">Querying Views<a href="#querying-views" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Querying Views" title="Direct link to Querying Views" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Views can be queried and subscribed to just like normal tables using SQL:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">SELECT</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> FROM</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> my_player;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">SELECT</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> FROM</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> players_for_level;</span></span></code></pre>
<p>When subscribed to, views automatically update when their underlying tables change, providing real-time updates to clients.</p>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="views-with-arguments">Views with Arguments<a href="#views-with-arguments" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Views with Arguments" title="Direct link to Views with Arguments" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>Views can accept arguments to select which data to return. For example, using the player level table defined above, this view returns player level rows at the requested level:</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><p>Pass a parameter object before the return type. The handler receives the arguments as an object after the context:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> playersAtLevel </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">anonymousView</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">players_at_level</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { level</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(playerLevels.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Array.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.db.playerLevels.level.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(level))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Add parameters after the context:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">PlayersAtLevel</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Name </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">players_at_level</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> List</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">PlayersAtLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Db.PlayerLevel.Level.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(level).</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ToList</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>Add parameters after the context:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> players_at_level, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> players_at_level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, level</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Vec</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player_level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(level)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">collect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><p>C++ modules do not yet support views with arguments.</p></div></div></div>
<p>Query or subscribe to the view by passing positional SQL literals in parameter order:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">SELECT</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> FROM</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> players_at_level(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">2</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre>
<blockquote>
<p>NOTE: When you subscribe to the same view with different arguments the client cache contains the union of the results, and the row callbacks for that view report changes from all subscribed arguments without identifying which arguments produced each row.</p>
</blockquote>
<p>Views that return a <a href="#query-builder-in-views" class="">query builder</a> can also take arguments. Anonymous views are shared among all subscribers that pass the same argument values.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_Ph9R alert alert--secondary"><div class="admonitionHeading_kG9t"><span class="admonitionIcon_VA_f"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"></path></svg></span>Client query builders</div><div class="admonitionContent_STxx"><p>TypeScript and C# client query builders do not yet support views with arguments. Use SQL strings instead. In the Rust client query builder, a view&#x27;s accessor takes its arguments directly.</p></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="limitations">Limitations<a href="#limitations" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Limitations" title="Direct link to Limitations" translate="no">​</a></h4>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><strong>SQL</strong>: Only <code>SELECT * FROM view_name(args)</code> is supported. Queries cannot add <code>WHERE</code> clauses, column lists, aggregates, or joins, so put any filtering inside the view.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Arguments</strong>: Arguments must be positional SQL literals that match the declared types. From SQL, parameters can be booleans, integers, strings, byte arrays, <code>Identity</code>, <code>ConnectionId</code>, <code>Timestamp</code>, or <code>Uuid</code>. Floating-point parameters, named arguments, and expressions are not supported.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Primary keys</strong>: Views with arguments cannot have a primary key. In TypeScript, primary-key metadata is automatically omitted from the returned row type.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Subscriptions</strong>: Subscribe to each view and set of arguments explicitly. Subscribe-all helpers such as <code>subscribeToAllTables</code>, <code>SubscribeToAllTables</code>, and <code>subscribe_to_all_tables</code> skip views with arguments.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="why-views-cannot-use-iter">Why Views Cannot Use <code>.iter()</code><a href="#why-views-cannot-use-iter" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to why-views-cannot-use-iter" title="Direct link to why-views-cannot-use-iter" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>You may notice that views can only access table data through indexed lookups (<code>.find()</code> and <code>.filter()</code> on indexed columns) and table-level metadata lookups like <code>count()</code>, not through <code>.iter()</code> which scans all rows. This is a deliberate design choice for performance.</p>
<p><strong>Views are black boxes.</strong> View functions are Turing-complete code that SpacetimeDB cannot analyze or optimize. When a view reads from a table, SpacetimeDB tracks the "read set" - which rows the view accessed. If any row in that read set changes, the view&#x27;s output might have changed, so SpacetimeDB must re-execute the entire view function.</p>
<p><strong>Full table scans create pessimistic read sets.</strong> If a view used <code>.iter()</code> to scan an entire table, its read set would include every row in that table. This means any change to any row - even rows unrelated to the view&#x27;s output - would trigger a complete re-evaluation of the view. For a table with millions of rows, this becomes prohibitively expensive.</p>
<p><strong>Index lookups enable targeted invalidation.</strong> When a view uses <code>.find()</code> or <code>.filter()</code> on an indexed column, SpacetimeDB knows exactly which rows the view depends on. If a row outside that set changes, the view doesn&#x27;t need to be re-evaluated. This keeps view updates fast and predictable.</p>
<p><strong>Why SQL subscriptions can scan.</strong> You might wonder why SQL subscription queries can include full table scans while view functions cannot. The difference is that SQL queries are not black boxes - SpacetimeDB can analyze and transform them. The query engine uses <strong>incremental evaluation</strong>: when rows change, it computes exactly which output rows are affected without re-running the entire query. Think of it like taking the derivative of the query - given a small change in input, compute the small change in output. Since view functions are opaque code, this kind of incremental computation isn&#x27;t possible.</p>
<p><strong>Why query builder subscriptions can scan.</strong> For the same reason that SQL subscriptions can scan. Anything you can do with SQL subscriptions you can do with the query builder API and vice versa.</p>
<p><strong>The tradeoff is acceptable for indexed access.</strong> For point lookups (<code>.find()</code>) and small range scans (<code>.filter()</code> on indexed columns), the performance difference between full re-evaluation and incremental evaluation is small. This is why views are limited to indexed access - it&#x27;s the subset of operations where the black-box limitation doesn&#x27;t hurt performance.</p>
<p>If you need to aggregate or sort entire tables, consider returning a <code>Query</code> from your view instead. Since queries can be analyzed by the query engine, they support incremental evaluation even when scanning full tables. Alternatively, design your schema so the data you need is accessible through indexes.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="performance-considerations">Performance Considerations<a href="#performance-considerations" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Performance Considerations" title="Direct link to Performance Considerations" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Views compute results server side, which can improve performance by:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">Reducing network traffic by filtering/aggregating before sending data</li>
<li class="">Avoiding redundant computation on multiple clients</li>
<li class="">Leveraging server-side indexes and query optimization</li>
</ul>
<p>However, keep in mind that:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">View functions are reevaluated when SpacetimeDB detects something they read has since changed (the read set)</li>
<li class="">The key to optimizing a view function is keeping its read set small</li>
<li class="">View functions can have large read sets when they read from non-unique indexes and/or multiple tables without using the query builder</li>
<li class="">View functions are executed largely as written by the WASM/V8 runtimes with little room for global optimization</li>
<li class="">Join-heavy view functions will incur extra row materialization costs when serializing data across the WASM/V8 boundary</li>
</ul>
<p>If a view is primarily performing query logic like filtering and joining rows, it is recommended to use the module-side query builder,
since it pushes work into the query engine, which can optimize and evaluate the plan more efficiently.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="query-builder-in-views">Query Builder in Views<a href="#query-builder-in-views" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Query Builder in Views" title="Direct link to Query Builder in Views" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="example">Example<a href="#example" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Example" title="Direct link to Example" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> highScorers </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">anonymousView</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">high_scorers</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(players.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from.players</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.name.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ne</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">BOT</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">HighScorers</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> IQuery</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">HighScorers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.From.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.Score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000UL</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.Name.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Neq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">BOT</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> high_scorers, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> high_scorers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> impl</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Query</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">from</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">r#where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">score</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">r#where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ne</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"BOT"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Query</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, high_scorers, Public, AnonymousViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from[player]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.name.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ne</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">BOT</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="why-use-the-query-builder">Why Use the Query Builder?<a href="#why-use-the-query-builder" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Why Use the Query Builder?" title="Direct link to Why Use the Query Builder?" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>Both <code>ViewContext</code> and <code>AnonymousViewContext</code> expose a builder API for expressing query logic like filters and joins.
The module-side query builder is designed for view logic that is mostly filters and joins.
These views are evaluated by the query engine instead of the WASM/V8 runtime, the benefits of which include the following:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">The query engine can apply global optimizations that WASM and V8 cannot, drastically improving the performance of your views</li>
<li class="">These views can be updated incrementally; the database does not have to re-evaluate the entire view if its read set changes</li>
<li class="">These views avoid the row materialization costs incurred by procedural view functions every time your code fetches/reads a row from the database</li>
</ul>
<p>For join-heavy views, this difference is often significant.</p>
<p>In the example below, we have <code>players</code> and <code>moderators</code>.
The procedural version must execute as written: iterate filtered rows from <code>players</code>, then probe <code>moderators</code> row by row.
The query builder version expresses the same relationship declaratively,
which means the query engine is free to choose a better plan,
including reordering the join and starting from <code>moderators</code> if it thinks that will be more efficient.</p>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">100</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">left_semijoin</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.from[moderator], [](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> m</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(m.player_id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="api-reference">API Reference<a href="#api-reference" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to API Reference" title="Direct link to API Reference" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="table-accessors">Table Accessors<a href="#table-accessors" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Table Accessors" title="Direct link to Table Accessors" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p>The query builder is available only through <code>ViewContext</code> and <code>AnonymousViewContext</code>:
There is an accessor method/property for each table defined in your module.</p>

<span class="line"></span>

<span class="line"></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[primary_key]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[auto_inc]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[index(btree)]</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player_level, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[unique]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    player_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[index(btree)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    level</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> all_players, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> all_players</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> impl</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Query</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">from</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> all_player_levels, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> all_player_levels</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> impl</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Query</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">from</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player_level</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> score;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Player, id, name, score)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Player, player, Public)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player, id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player, score)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player_id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> level;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(PlayerLevel, player_id, level)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(PlayerLevel, player_level, Public)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Unique</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player_level, player_id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(player_level, level)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Query</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, all_players, Public, AnonymousViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from[player];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Query</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, all_player_levels, Public, AnonymousViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from[player_level];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="where--filter"><code>where</code> / <code>filter</code><a href="#where--filter" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to where--filter" title="Direct link to where--filter" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p>Use <code>where</code> to apply predicates. Chaining multiple filters combines them with logical <code>AND</code>.</p>
<p><code>filter</code> is an alias for <code>where</code> in Rust and C#.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="comparison-operators">Comparison operators<a href="#comparison-operators" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Comparison operators" title="Direct link to Comparison operators" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<table><thead><tr><th>Operation</th><th>Rust</th><th>TypeScript</th><th>C#</th></tr></thead>\<tbody\><tr><td>Equal</td><td><code>eq</code></td><td><code>eq</code></td><td><code>Eq</code></td></tr><tr><td>Not equal</td><td><code>ne</code></td><td><code>ne</code></td><td><code>Neq</code></td></tr><tr><td>Less than</td><td><code>lt</code></td><td><code>lt</code></td><td><code>Lt</code></td></tr><tr><td>Less than or equal</td><td><code>lte</code></td><td><code>lte</code></td><td><code>Lte</code></td></tr><tr><td>Greater than</td><td><code>gt</code></td><td><code>gt</code></td><td><code>Gt</code></td></tr><tr><td>Greater than or equal</td><td><code>gte</code></td><td><code>gte</code></td><td><code>Gte</code></td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> highScorers </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">anonymousView</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">high_scorers</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(players.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from.players</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.name.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ne</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">BOT</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">HighScorers</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> IQuery</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">HighScorers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.From.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.Score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000UL</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.Name.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Neq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">BOT</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> high_scorers, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> high_scorers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> impl</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Query</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">from</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">r#where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">score</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ne</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"BOT"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Query</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, high_scorers, Public, AnonymousViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from[player]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.name.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ne</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">BOT</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="boolean-combinators">Boolean combinators<a href="#boolean-combinators" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Boolean combinators" title="Direct link to Boolean combinators" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<p>Combine conditions with <code>and</code>, <code>or</code>, and <code>not</code>:</p>

<p>Comparisons are strongly typed, meaning invalid comparisons are rejected by the type system.
An example would be comparing an <code>Identity</code> column to an integer literal.</p>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="semijoins">Semijoins<a href="#semijoins" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Semijoins" title="Direct link to Semijoins" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p>Semijoins keep rows from one side when a matching row exists on the other side. They are used for filtering rows in one table based on rows in the other table.</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">A left semijoin returns rows from the left side that have at least one match on the right side.</li>
<li class="">A right semijoin returns rows from the right side that have at least one match on the left side.</li>
<li class="">Filters chained before a semijoin apply to the pre-join source side.</li>
<li class="">Filters chained after a semijoin apply to whichever side the semijoin returns.</li>
<li class="">Like <code>where/filter</code>, join predicates are strongly typed</li>
<li class="">Additionally join predicates may only use indexed columns with multi-column indexes not supported at the moment</li>
</ul>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> playersWithLevels </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">anonymousView</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">players_with_levels</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(players.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from.players</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">leftSemijoin</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.from.playerLevels, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">pl</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(pl.playerId));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> levelsForHighScorers </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">anonymousView</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">levels_for_high_scorers</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from.players</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.score.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1000</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">rightSemijoin</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.from.playerLevels, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">pl</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(pl.playerId))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">pl</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pl.level.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">gte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">10</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">PlayersWithLevels</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> IQuery</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">PlayersWithLevels</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AnonymousViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.From.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">LeftSemijoin</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.From.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">PlayerLevel</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">p</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">pl</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> p.Id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(pl.PlayerId));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
