<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="primary-keys-for-views">Primary Keys for Views<a href="#primary-keys-for-views" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Primary Keys for Views" title="Direct link to Primary Keys for Views" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>Views can have primary-key semantics when SpacetimeDB knows which column identifies each returned row.
Generated client bindings treat these views like primary-key tables for client-cache updates.
In particular, update callbacks (<code>on_update</code> / <code>OnUpdate</code> / <code>onUpdate</code>) are generated for these view handles.
Views without a known primary key fall back to insert/delete-only behavior.</p>
<p>For procedural views, primary keys are declared in the view definition for Rust and C#, and with a post-view declaration macro in C++.
In TypeScript, you mark one of the columns in the returned row type with <code>.primaryKey()</code>.
View primary keys refer to source/accessor names, not case-converted or canonical database column names.
The examples below use a <code>Player</code> row with a primary-key <code>id</code> / <code>Id</code> column and an indexed <code>owner</code> / <code>Owner</code> column.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { schema, table, t } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb/server</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> players </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">players</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">primaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">autoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    owner</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">btree</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> schema</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ players });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> default</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> myPlayers </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">view</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my_players</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(players.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Array.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.db.players.owner.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">MyPlayers</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, PrimaryKey </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Id</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> IEnumerable</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">MyPlayers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Db.Player.Owner.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.Sender);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[view(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> my_players, public, primary_key </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> id)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> my_players</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ViewContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Vec</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">owner</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">collect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">vector</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Player</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, my_players, Public, ViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[player_owner].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()).</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">collect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">VIEW_PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(my_players, id)</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>Query builder views may also carry primary-key semantics from their underlying row type.
When a query builder view&#x27;s row type maps to a table with a primary key,
generated client bindings can treat the view like a primary-key table for client-cache updates.</p>
<p>A view result must not contain two rows with the same primary key.
If a view returns duplicate primary key values during a view refresh, SpacetimeDB rejects that view result and fails the transaction that triggered the refresh.</p>
